- **styleshift-hemingway**: Activates Hemingway writing mode. - **styleshift-limerick**: Switches the conversation to limerick style. Trigger when the user types `/limerick` or `/mode limerick`. Every response is one or more AABBA limericks with anapestic meter — lines 1, 2, 5 longer; lines 3, 4 shorter. Line 5 must deliver a punchline, twist, or callback. ([LkwdBrian/styleshift](https://github.com/LkwdBrian/styleshift/tree/HEAD/modes/limerick))
- **styleshift-noir**: Switches the conversation to hard-boiled noir narration style. Trigger when the user types `/noir` or `/mode noir`. - **sub-agent**: COSTLY: Spawns a separate sub-agent CLI session. Use ONLY for complex CODE tasks requiring 10+ iterative tool calls (multi-file refactoring with tests, code review with fixes, test-fix cycles). IMPORTANT: Always run the model discovery script first before any sub_agent call. Do NOT use for presentations, research, documentation, or any task completable in fewer than 10 tool calls. ([Wide-Moat/open-computer-use](https://github.com/Wide-Moat/open-computer-use/tree/HEAD/skills/public/sub-agent) · ★126)
- **sub-agent**: Route coding work to an external coding harness (Claude Code, Codex, Gemini CLI, Cursor, GitHub Copilot, OpenCode, Pi, Droid, Qwen, Kimi, Kiro, ...) as an ACP child session via sessions_spawn(runtime="acp"). Use when: the user names a coding agent ("have Codex fix this", "ask Claude Code to review"), wants a feature built, a PR reviewed, a refactor or a multi-file change done by a separate worker, or a meta-skill step needs a full coding agent. Read this before spawning, steering, waiting for, or cancelling a harness. NOT for: one-line edits (edit directly), reading code (use read tools), or… ([use-agent-os/agent-os](https://github.com/use-agent-os/agent-os/tree/HEAD/src/agentos/skills/bundled/sub-agent) · ★81)
- **sub-agent**: Delegate work to another agent: `sub_agent` (default — spawns a child task and wait for its answer), `sub_agent_continue` (follow up on a sub-agent task you already started, and wait for the new answer), or `handover` (the target works in the background; you get no answer back). Trigger on: 'delegate this', 'spawn a sub-agent', 'hand off to N', 'let N take it from here', 'send it to N', 'use the N agent for this'. The critical rules: (a) `sub_agent` is the default — only `handover` when the source agent does NOT need to hear back; (b) for `sub_agent` and `handover` the `prompt` is the ONLY in… ([spora-ai/spora-core](https://github.com/spora-ai/spora-core/tree/HEAD/skills/sub-agent) · ★1)
